# Appendix — Full finding list from the automated review (Ch. 1–17, all appendices, front matter, cross-cutting checks)

Each finding was produced by one of 18 reviewer agents (two lenses per chunk for Ch. 1–17 and the appendices, plus eight book-wide checks). Every quoted passage below was mechanically confirmed to exist in the named file; the *problem/fix* text is the reviewer's own and has **not** been through the second (adversarial) verification pass, except where the main report says so. Treat `high` items as very likely real, `medium` as probable, `low` as editorial suggestions. Line numbers are approximate (±2).

## 03-microbenchmarking-done-right.md  (18 findings)

| Sev | Line | Category | Quote | Problem | Suggested fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| high | 154 | technical-inaccuracy | at `N = 4096` with random keys the flat array doesn't fit in L1, so this is not the cache-friendly regime where flat structures shine | 4096 × sizeof(uint32_t) = 16 KiB, which fits comfortably in the reference machine's 48 KiB L1d (Ch. 7 line 143; even Skylake's 32 KiB). It is the std::unordered_map (~4096 separately-malloc'd 16–32 B nodes + a ~4096×8 B bucket array ≈ 150–200 KiB) that does *not* fit L1. | medium | 17 | technical-inaccuracy | The same 64-entry TLB that reached 256 KB with 4 KB pages reaches **128 MB** with 2 MB pages | On Intel cores (Skylake through Sunny Cove / Ice Lake-SP) the L1 load-DTLB is split by page size: 64 entries for 4 KB pages, but only 32 entries for 2 MB/4 MB pages (and 8 for 1 GB). The 64 entries do not become 2 MB entries, so the L1-dTLB reach with 2 MB pages is 32 x 2 MB = 64 MB, not 128 MB. The chapter even notes 'separate sub-pools for 4 KB and 2 MB pages' in 15.2.2 and then ignores it. Verified against Wiki… | Say: 'the 32-entry 2 MB sub-pool of the L1 dTLB reaches 64 MB (vs 256 KB for the 64 x 4 KB entries), and the STLB reaches ~2 GB' and give the per-size entry counts (4K: 64 / 2M: 32 / 1G: 8) once in 15.2.2. |
| medium | 51 | technical-inaccuracy | dTLB (64 entries)   64 × 4 KB  = 256 KB     64 × 2 MB  = 128 MB    STLB (2048)         2048 × 4 KB = 8 MB       2048 × 2 MB = 4 GB | Same error as above in the reach table, plus the STLB row: on Sunny Cove the 2048-entry STLB is partitioned so that 4 KB pages can use all 2048 entries but 2 MB/4 MB pages (and 1 GB pages) get at most 1024 entries each (shared with 4 KB). So the 2 MB STLB reach is ~1024 x 2 MB = 2 GB, not 4 GB, and the dTLB row should be 32 x 2 MB = 64 MB. The 'knees' predicted in Exercise 2 follow from these numbers. | Rewrite the table as: dTLB 4K: 64 x 4 KB = 256 KB; dTLB 2M: 32 x 2 MB = 64 MB; STLB 4K: 2048 x 4 KB = 8 MB; STLB 2M: ~1024 x 2 MB = 2 GB (Ice Lake-SP/Sunny Cove; cite Intel ORM / WikiChip), and keep the '512x per entry' point, which is correct. |
